Let's design robust functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def get_next_pow2(n):
    if n <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()

def fast_autocorr_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = n + n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()  # if size=1? (0 bit_length 0)=1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, fsize)
    return c[:size]

def fast_max_scores(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0: return (np.inf, np.inf, None)
    c=fast_autocorr_full(a)
    # first half enough
    max_full = float(np.max(c[:n]))
    max_off = float(np.max(c[1:n])) if n>1 else 0.0
    inv = n/(S*S)
    return max_full*inv, max_off*inv, c
```
But fast_autocorr uses A*A with no conjugate? For real rfft, A is complex; `A*A` should be `A * np.conjugate(A)` = |A|^2. Since A from real signal is complex; product A*A without conjugate is wrong! Previous code used `A * A`. For real FFT, A[k] and A[-k] conjugates; to get power spectrum need A * np.conj(A). If they use A*A, is that a bug? Let's check: For real input, rfft coefficients: A[0] real, last Nyquist real, interior complex. |A|^2 = A * conj(A). If they use A*A, then irFFT of A^2 gives circular convolution? Wait DFT of a*a (circular) would be A^2, not autocorr. For autocorr (nonnegative linear) need A * conj(A). Using A*A computes linear convolution? No, circular convolution of a with itself has DFT A^2. For real a, if a is not symmetric, A^2 not equal power. Example a=[1,2], n=2, size=3 fsize4? A? A^2 inverse not autocorr. Previous code may have bug! They used `A * A` instead of `A * A.conjugate()`. That could produce wrong conv, potentially negative, and max maybe lower, explaining impossible scores! Aha! The previous internal `fast_autocorr` is buggy; but safe_eval uses np.convolve correct? If they used fast_autocorr for internal and safe_eval only occasional, they might have found sequences with low *buggy* internal score but actual safe_eval high? But previous reported score from safe_eval? They call safe_eval final; but if safe_eval proper, no. However, if actual evaluate_sequence maybe uses same bug? It uses np.convolve, not.

Wait DFT convention: Autocorrelation c_k = sum_i a_i a_{i+k}. This is not circular convolution of a with a? Linear convolution (a*a)_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. For same sequence, c_k for positive lags equals (a * reversed?) Not direct convolution. But for same sequence, linear convolution b_t = sum_i a_i a_{t-i}. Autocorrelation with lag k = sum_i a_i a_{i+k} = b_{2k?} not. Actually convolution of sequence with itself yields b_t = sum_i a_i a_{t-i}. For t=0, b0=sum a_i a_{-i}=0 unless negative indices? In linear conv with indexing 0..n-1, b_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. For k=0, requires i=0 and k-i=0 ->a0^2? Wait convolution formula: (a*b)_k = sum_{i=0}^{n-1} a_i b_{k-i}, where b_j=0 if j outside 0..n-1. For a=b, coefficient for index k (0..2n-2) = sum_{i=max(0,k-n+1)}^{min(n-1,k)} a_i a_{k-i}. This is symmetric and equals sum over pairs i+j=k. Autocorrelation lag d = sum_i a_i a_{i+d}. That is sum over pairs with difference d, not sum. How relates? Convolution with reversed a: (a * a[::-1]) yields autocorr. But they use np.convolve(sequence, sequence), which computes sum over i+j=k, not difference! For nonnegative, the max of self-convolution (sum of pairs summing to k) is not autocorrelation lag! Cauchy c0? For self-convolution b_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. The maximum coefficient at 2n-2? Let's analyze. For nonnegative, b_{n-1} central = sum_{i=0}^{n-1} a_i a_{n-1-i}. There's no direct lower bound by S^2/n? Parseval for convolution: sum_k b_k = S^2. Max b_k >= average = S^2/(2n-1). So objective >= 2n/(2n-1) ~2, not 2. Uniform: all a=1, b_k = k+1 for k<n, max at n-1 = n. S=n, objective 2n*n/n^2=2. Lower bound slightly 2n/(2n-1) ~2. Parent 1.504 still impossible? For half metric lower bound n/(2n-1) ~0.5. Ramp? Let's compute half objective for self-convolution linear (sum pairs) for linear ramp. S~n^2/2. b_k for central? For a_i~i, b_k = sum_{i} i(i+k) with k fixed? For k ~ n, maybe ~? Maximum at central k=n-1: sum_{i=1}^{n-1} i(n-i) ~n^3/6. S^2~n^4/4. n*b/S^2 ~ n*(n^3/6)/(n^4/4)=2/3. Provided 4/3? But previous comment ramp J->4/3? Maybe for autocorr difference. Let's detail functions:
```
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 1
def fft_conv(a, fft_size=None):
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    if fft_size is None: fft_size=next_pow2(size)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    C=A*A  # not conjugate
    b=np.fft.irfft(C, fft_size)
    # due to roundoff, tiny negatives; clip
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)  # Is that safe? For nonnegative conv true nonnegative; clipping small negative ok. But if bug? ok.
    return b[:size], fft_size
```
Need check memory: b full N. ok.
`score_pref(a)`: ensure sum=1. Return J, b, fft_size maybe.
`objective_from_b(b,n)`: 2.0*n*max(b[:size])? Conv full symmetric? We need full length 2n-1; max all. Could take b.max().

For gradient and active lags, we need b length 2n-1. Use top indices. We can find top by `np.argpartition`.

`compute_grad(a, ks, weights, n)`:
```
grad=np.zeros(n)
for k,w in zip(ks,weights):
    k=int(k); w=float(w)
    if w<=0: continue
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    if lo>hi: continue
    # pair_idx = k - np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    # grad[lo:hi+1] += 2*w*a[pair_idx]
    grad[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*w*a[k-hi:k-lo-1:-1]  # because when j=lo pair=k-lo (high); when j=hi pair=k-hi (low)
```

import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(m): ...
def _conv_and_score(a):
    # returns J, b
    a = np.asarray(a, float)
    n=len(a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf, None
    if abs(S-1)>1e-9: a=a/S
    size=2*n-1
    N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    C=A*A
    b=np.fft.irfft(C,N)[:size]
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    J=2.0*n*float(np.max(b))
    return J,b
```

Implement `spectral_update(a)`: one or batch.
```
def spectral_step(a, N, target_ratio=0.95, alpha=0.5):
    n=len(a)
    b_full = zeros(N); A=fft(a,N); C=A*A; b=ifft(C).real[:2n-1]
    M=b.max(); avg=1/(2n-1)
    # target plateau = max(avg*target_flat, M*0.5?) But to reduce, choose target= max(M*0.8, avg*1.6)
    target = max(0.7*M, avg*1.5 + (M-avg)*0.1)
    over = np.maximum(b-target,0)
    b_new = b - alpha*over
    # ensure nonneg
    b_new=np.maximum(b_new,0)
    s=b_new.sum(); if s>0: b_new/=s  # because sum must 1
    # circular tail zeros
    bf=np.zeros(N); bf[:2n-1]=b_new
    Ct=fft(bf)
    # make Ct[0] positive: sum=1 => Ct[0]=1
    mag=np.sqrt(np.abs(Ct)); ph=np.angle(Ct)
    At=mag*np.exp(0.5j*ph)
    # enforce Hermitian? fft of real bf already Hermitian; sqrt branch with angle ensures? For real input, Ct[-k]=conj(Ct[k]); angle(-k)=-angle, so yes. But roundoff.
    At[0]=np.sqrt(max(Ct[0].real,0))
    a_new=ifft(At,N).real[:n]
    a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,None); S=a_new.sum(); if S<1e-12: return a
    return a_new/S
```
But sum of b_new normalized to1 means Ct[0]=1? Only if bf length N with zeros tail, sum bf=1 -> fft[0]=1 (real). Good.

Spectral step using rfft:
```
def spectral_step_rfft(a, N, alpha=0.3):
    n=len(a)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    C = A*A
    b = np.fft.irfft(C,N)[:2*n-1]
    M=b.max(); avg=1/(2*n-1)
    target = M - 0.25*(M-avg)  # reduce peaks by 25%
    over = np.maximum(b-target,0)
    b_new = b - alpha*over
    b_new = np.maximum(b_new,0)
    s=b_new.sum()
    if s>0: b_new/=s
    bf = np.zeros(N); bf[:2*n-1]=b_new
    Cpos = np.fft.rfft(bf,N)
    Cpos[0]=1.0
    if N%2==0:
        Cpos[-1] = abs(Cpos[-1])
    mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(Cpos))
    phase = np.angle(Cpos)
    Apos = mag * (np.cos(phase/2) + 1j*np.sin(phase/2))
    Apos[0]=1.0
    a_new = np.fft.irfft(Apos,N)[:n]
    a_new = np.clip(a_new,0,None)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return a, currentJ
    return a_new/s, None
```
Then score. We can blend if not improve: `a = normalize(0.8*a + 0.2*a_new)` to gather.
Adaptive alpha: start0.2, if plateau increase to1.

But average is0.5, target ~? For J=1.57 M=0.785/n, avg=0.5/n, 0.6M=0.471/n < avg, maybe too aggressive. Because average is 0.5/n, max cannot be below avg. ```
def active_weights(b, M, n_peaks=60, peak_frac=0.05):
    vals=b
    # maybe include all above (1-peak_frac)M
    thresh = M - peak_frac*M
    idx = np.flatnonzero(b >= thresh)
    if len(idx)>n_peaks: top = np.argpartition(b, -n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]
    else: top=idx
    w = np.exp(-(M-b[top])/(M*0.05+1e-30))
    return top,w/w.sum()
```

```
def mirror_step(a, b, N, q, n_peaks=80):
   M=b.max()
   idx,w=...
   grad = compute_grad(a,idx,w)
   gmax=grad.max()
   if gmax<=0: return a
   loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - q*grad/gmax
   exp = np.exp(loga - loga.max())
   a_new = exp/sum; if sum<... return a
   return a_new
```

def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
        arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
        if arr.size==0: return inf
        # To avoid sum<0.01? if sum small normalize? Eval scale invariant, but for safety scale to sum=1 if finite
        s=arr.sum()
        if s < 1e-12: return inf
        arr=arr/s
        # ensure max <=1000, since sum1 max≤1
        lst=[float(x) for x in arr]
        val=evaluate_sequence(lst)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return inf
```

`interp_length`:
```
def interpolate_length(a,new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
    if n==new_n: return a
    s=a.sum(); if s<...: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    a=a/s
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    y=np.maximum(y,0); s2=y.sum(); if s2<...: return ones
    return y/s2
```

def conv_coeff(a,N):
   n=len(a)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
   if b.size: b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
   return b

def active_lags_weights(b, n_top=40, rel_tol=0.1):
    M=float(np.max(b))
    size=len(b)
    thresh=M*(1.0-rel_tol)
    idx=np.flatnonzero(b >= thresh)
    if idx.size > n_top:
       # select top among idx
       part=np.argpartition(-b, min(n_top, size)-1)???
       top=np.argpartition(b, -n_top)[-n_top:]
    else:
       top=idx
    if top.size==0: top=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    vals=b[top]
    scale=max(M*0.05, 1e-300)
    w=np.exp((vals-M)/scale)
    w=w/w.sum()
    return top.astype(int), w, M
```

`compute_gradient` vectorized:
```
def compute_gradient(a, ks, ws):
    n=len(a)
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(ks,ws):
       wi=float(w)
       if wi <= 0: continue
       ki=int(k)
       lo=ki - (n-1)
       if lo <0: lo=0
       hi=ki
       if hi>=n: hi=n-1
       if lo>hi: continue
       # pair slice descending
       # pair_start=ki - hi, pair_end=ki - lo
       grad[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wi*a[ki-hi : ki-lo-1 : -1]
    return grad
```

    best=a.copy(); bestJ=...

       grad=compute_gradient(a,top,w)

       # adaptive q
       q_eff=q

       else:

           # try smaller

           J2=...

`mirror_exp_update`:
```
def mirror_exp_update(a, grad, q):
    n=len(a)
    gmax=grad.max()
    if gmax <=0: return a
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - q*(grad/gmax)
    loga -= loga.max()
    a_new = np.exp(loga)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-30)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s <1e-30: return a
    a_new /= s
    return a_new
```
This change may make many a_i extremely small. Add floor: after normalize, `a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12/n)`? But if we force floor, changes objective. Maybe not.

Sigma noise:
```
def add_noise(a, rng, sigma=0.05):
    if a.size ==0: return a
    p=rng.normal(0, sigma, a.size)
    a_new=a*(1+p)
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s<1e-30: return a
    return a_new/s
```

Spectral phase more promising; implement with stopping if bestJ close to lower target? We don't know. Stop if no improvement 200 steps or target average ratio <1.51? Maybe.

Main time plan:
```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global=time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(float(budget_s)-60.0, 1740.0)  # leave 60 for final
    # collect candidates
    parent=...
    lengths=[]
    parent_n = len(parent) if parent else 5200
    for n in [2048,3500,parent_n,5200,7000]:
       if n not in lengths and 512<=n<=9000: lengths.append(n)
    seeds = make_seeds(parent, lengths)
    scored=[]
    for a in seeds:
       J,b,N=fast_score(a)
       scored.append((J,a,N))
    scored sort by J
    # Evaluate parent safe? maybe later.
    # choose top 10
    top=[] ensure parent, uniform included
    # main loop
    t0=time.monotonic()
    time_remaining = time_budget - (t0-t_global)
    n_main = min(10, len(top))
    # allocate time: first 4 get 240s, rest 80s? adjust to remaining.
    for i, (J,a,N) in enumerate(top):

```
def normalize_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return None
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-15: return None
   return a/s
```

```
def spectral_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N):
   a=norm; J,b=score; best...
   cap_frac=0.95; stall=0; it=0
   while time<deadline:
      M=b.max(); avg=1/(2n-1)
      # cap_frac adapt: if stall>50: cap_frac=min(0.9, cap_frac-0.02); if stall>150: cap_frac=0.98
      t=max(avg, cap_frac*M)
      b_cap=np.minimum(b,t); s=b_cap.sum(); if s>0: b_cap/=s
      a_new=spectral_from_b(b_cap,N,n)
      J_new,b_new=score(a_new,N)
      if J_new < J_cur:
          a=a_new; b=b_new; J_cur=J_new; if J_new<best: best; stall=0; cap_frac=max(0.85, cap_frac-0.01) # push harder
      else:
          # blend
          blend=0.25
          a_blend=normalize( (1-blend)*a + blend*a_new )
          J_blend,b_blend=score(a_blend,N)
          if J_blend < J_cur:
              a=a_blend; b=b_blend; J_cur=J_blend; cap_frac=max(0.85, cap_frac-0.01)
          else:
              stall+=1
              cap_frac=min(0.99, cap_frac+0.02)
              if stall>100: a=add_noise; recompute; stall=0
   return best
```

Now, final code details.

One more mathematical idea: Since C = A^2, and b nonnegative, the phase of A can be chosen; our spectral square root uses principal branch. Could improve by choosing phase sign to make inverse more nonnegative. For each frequency, A can be ±sqrt(C). We choose e^{iθ/2}. Could also multiply high frequencies by -1? Hard. Projection handles.

Potential problem: In spectral step, capping b to t and normalizing to sum1 may make b negative? no. `buf[:size]=b_cap` zeros tail; if b_cap sum1, Cpos[0]=1. After square root and inverse, a may have negative; after clip sum might be much less; normalize. Good.

Let's consider if `A*A` with rfft for current b uses unnormalized FFT. Convolution coefficients from `irfft(A*A)` with default FFT normalization: DFT unnormalized, inverse 1/N. If A=fft(a), A*A inverse gives N*(a*a) circular? Let's check numpy: fft unnormalized: X_k=∑ x_n e^{-2πi...}; ifft(Y)=1/N∑ Y_k e^{...}. For convolution, ifft(fft(a)*fft(a)) = circular convolution a*a exactly (no extra N). Good. Zero-padded to N≥2n-1 gives linear. b sum= (sum a)^2. Good.

Fast score with sum1: average coefficient =1/(2n-1), not 1/N due zeros tail. But b length 2n-1; OK.

In spectral cap, we sum b_cap over size 2n-1 and normalize to1; zero tail sum0. Good.

Potential issue: `np.maximum(b,0.0)` in `conv_coeff` may hide invalid negative b from square root step; but true nonnegative. ok.

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`convoke_score` maybe returns `J,b,N` with b clipped. Use:
```
def fast_score(a, N=None):
    n = len(a)
    if N is None:
       N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    if b.size:
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    return 2.0*n*M, b
```
N not returned to avoid? We can compute separately.

`active_lags_weights(b,n_top,rel_tol)`:
```
def active_lags_weights(b, n_top, rel_tol):
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    sz=b.size
    if sz ==0: return np.array([0]), np.array([1.0]), M
    if n_top > sz: n_top=sz
    if n_top <=1:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
       return idx, np.array([1.0]), M
    top=np.argpartition(b, -n_top)[-n_top:]
    # optionally keep only if b > M*(1-rel_tol)
    thresh=M*(1.0-rel_tol)
    keep=top[b[top] >= thresh]
    if keep.size ==0:
       keep=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    elif keep.size > n_top:
       keep=keep[:n_top]
    vals=b[keep]
    scale=max(1e-300, M*0.05)
    w=np.exp((vals-M)/scale)
    wsum=float(w.sum())
    if wsum <=0 or not np.isfinite(wsum): w=np.ones(keep.size)/keep.size
    else: w=w/wsum
    return keep.astype(int), w, M
```
If b all equal, all vals M, w uniform. Good.

`compute_gradient` as above.

`mirror_update`:
```
def mirror_update(a, grad, q):
    gmax=float(np.max(grad))
    if gmax <= 1e-300: return a.copy()
    loga=np.log(a + 1e-300) - q * (grad/gmax)
    loga=loga - float(np.max(loga))
    a_new=np.exp(loga)
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
    s=float(a_new.sum())
    if s <1e-300: return a.copy()
    return a_new/s
```

`add_noise`:
```
def add_noise(a, rng, sigma):
    p = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, len(a))
    a_new = a * (1.0 + p)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    s = a_new.sum()
    if s <1e-300: return a
    return a_new/s
```

`spectral_from_b`:
```
def spectral_from_b(b_cap, N, n):
    size=2*n-1
    if len(b_cap) < size:
       temp=np.zeros(size); temp[:len(b_cap)]=b_cap; b_cap=temp
    s=float(np.sum(b_cap))
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    b_cap=b_cap/s
    buf=np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
    buf[:size]=b_cap
    Cpos=np.fft.rfft(buf,N)
    Cpos[0]=1.0+0j
    if N % 2 ==0:
       Cpos[-1]=abs(Cpos[-1]) + 0j
    mag=np.sqrt(np.abs(Cpos))
    ph=np.angle(Cpos)
    Apos=mag*(np.cos(0.5*ph)+1j*np.sin(0.5*ph))
    Apos[0]=1.0+0j
    a=np.fft.irfft(Apos,N)[:n]
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s2=float(a.sum())
    if s2 <1e-300: return None
    return a/s2
```
`np.angle` on complex array ok.

`spectral_phase`:
```
def spectral_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N):
   a=normalize_sequence(a0)
   if a is None: return None, inf
   n=len(a)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   J,b = fast_score(a,N)
   best=a.copy(); bestJ=J
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   cap_frac=0.95
   stall=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      M=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
      avg=1.0/(2*n-1)
      t_target=max(avg, cap_frac*M)
      if t_target >= M:
         t_target = max(avg, 0.99*M) if M>avg else M
      b_cap=np.minimum(b, t_target)
      b_cap=np.maximum(b_cap,0.0)
      a_new=spectral_from_b(b_cap,N,n)
      if a_new is not None:
         J_new,b_new=fast_score(a_new,N)
         if J_new < J - 1e-12:
            a=a_new; b=b_new; J=J_new
            if J < bestJ - 1e-12: best=a.copy(); bestJ=J; stall=0
            cap_frac=max(0.82, cap_frac-0.01)
         else:
            blend=0.25
            a_blend=normalize_sequence((1.0-blend)*a + blend*a_new)
            if a_blend is not None:
               J_blend,b_blend=fast_score(a_blend,N)
               if J_blend < J - 1e-12:
                  a=a_blend; b=b_blend; J=J_blend
                  if J < bestJ -1e-12: best=a.copy(); bestJ=J; stall=0
               else:
                  stall +=1
                  cap_frac=min(0.99, cap_frac+0.015)
                  if stall > 80:
                     cap_frac=0.95; stall=0; it=0
                     a=add_noise(a,rng,0.03); J,b=fast_score(a,N)
                     if J < bestJ: bestJ=J; best=a.copy()
            else:
               stall +=1
      else:
         stall +=1
      it +=1
      if it % 200 ==0 and verbose? no.
   return best, bestJ
```
If current J initial is `J` but after noise maybe best? ok.

`mirror_phase` similar.

def mirror_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N, n_peaks=35):
    a=normalize; J,b=fast; best...
    rng=...
    q=0.6; stall=0; it=0
    while time < deadline:
       M=...
       top,w,_=active_lags_weights(b,n_peaks,0.2)
       grad=compute_gradient(a,top,w)
       gmax=grad.max()
       if gmax <=1e-300: break
       a_new=mirror_update(a, grad, q)
       J_new,b_new=fast_score(a_new,N)
       if J_new < J - 1e-12:
          a=a_new; b=b_new; J=J_new
          if J < bestJ - 1e-12: best=a.copy(); bestJ=J; stall=0
          q = min(4.0, q*1.04)
       else:
          q = max(0.05, q*0.85)
          stall +=1
          if stall > 400:
             a=add_noise(a,rng,0.04); J,b=fast_score(a,N); stall=0; q=0.6
             if J < bestJ: bestJ=J; best=a.copy()
       it +=1
       if it % 1000 ==0 and verbose...
    return best,bestJ
```

`coordinate_phase`: implement moderate.
```
def coordinate_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N, n_moves=5):
    a=norm; J,b=fast; best...
    rng=...
    stall=0
    while time<deadline:
       k0=int(np.argmax(b))
       lo=max(0,k0-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k0)
       idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       size=idx.size
       if size<2: break
       prod=a[idx]*a[k0-idx]
       src_pos=int(np.argmax(prod))
       src=idx[src_pos]
       # pick 200 low prod positions via argpartition
       sample_size=min(200,size-1)
       if sample_size>0:
          low=np.argpartition(prod, sample_size)[:sample_size]
          # choose one not src
          candidates=[]
          for pos in low:
             d=idx[pos]
             if d != src: candidates.append(d)
          if candidates:
             dst=candidates[rng.integers(0,len(candidates))]
          else: continue
       else: continue
       moved=False
       for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5):
           delta=a[src]*frac
           if delta <=0: continue
           a_t=a.copy(); a_t[src]-=delta; a_t[dst]+=delta
           a_t=np.maximum(a_t,0); s=t.sum(); if s<... continue; a_t/=s
           J_t=fast_score(a_t,N)[0]
           if J_t < bestJ - 1e-12:
               a=a_t; J=J_t; bestJ=J_t; best=a.copy(); stall=0; moved=True; break
       if not moved:
           # random pair move
           i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
           if i != j and a[i] > 0:
              delta=a[i]*rng.uniform(0.01,0.08)
              a_r=a.copy(); a_r[i]-=delta; a_r[j]+=delta; normalize
              J_r=fast_score(a_r,N)[0]
              if J_r < J - 1e-12:
                  a=a_r; J=J_r; stall=0
              else:
                  stall +=1
                  if stall > 200: a=add_noise(a,rng,0.05); J=fast_score(a,N)[0]; stall=0
          else: stall +=1
    return best,bestJ
```
This is expensive due many a_t copies and conv. Limit to small remaining. Good.

def optimize_candidate(a0, t_limit, seed):
    if t_limit <=5: return a0, fast_score...
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline_end=t0+max(0.0,t_limit)
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a is None: return None, inf
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=a; bestJ=fast_score(a,N)[0]
    # spectral
    spec_alloc = min(0.45*t_limit, 180.0)
    spec_deadline = min(t0+spec_alloc, deadline_end)
    if time.monotonic() < spec_deadline:
       a_s,J_s=spectral_phase(a,spec_deadline,seed,N)
       if a_s is not None and J_s<bestJ: best=a_s; bestJ=J_s
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
    rem = t_limit - elapsed
    if rem > 20:
       mir_alloc = min(0.5*rem, 240.0)
       mir_deadline = min(time.monotonic()+mir_alloc, deadline_end)
       a_m,J_m=mirror_phase(best,mir_deadline,seed+101,N)
       if J_m<bestJ: best=a_m; bestJ=J_m
    rem = t_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem > 15:
       coord_deadline = min(time.monotonic()+min(0.3*rem, 80.0), deadline_end)
       a_c,J_c=coordinate_phase(best,coord_deadline,seed+202,N)
       if J_c<bestJ: best=a_c; bestJ=J_c
    return best,bestJ
```

`make_candidate_pool(parent, lengths)`: We need deterministic seeds. Use top-level.
```
def make_candidate_pool(parent, lengths):
    pool=[]
    # parent
    if parent is not None:
       pool.append(parent)
       pn=len(parent)
       for mult in (0.5,1.5):
           nn=int(pn*mult)
           if 512 <= nn <= 10000:
               pool.append(interpolate_length(parent, nn))
    for n in lengths:
       pool.append(np.ones(n)/n)
       x=np.arange(1.0,n+1)
       right=np.arange(n,0.0,-1.0)
       # endpoint powers
       for l in (0.0,0.2,0.35,0.5):
          left=x**(-l)
          for r in (-0.5,-0.25,0.0,0.25,0.5):
             a=left * (right**r)
             aa=normalize_sequence(a)
             if aa is not None: pool.append(aa)
       # polynomial ramps
       for p in (0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
          a=x**p
          pool.append(normalize_sequence(a))
       # random smooth
       rng=np.random.default_rng(100+n)
       for _ in range(3):
          u=rng.random(n)
          a = -np.log(1-u) * x  # product with ramp
          pool.append(normalize_sequence(a))
    # deduplicate? Could be many but okay.
    return [a for a in pool if a is not None]
```
Random `-log(1-u)*x` may be huge but normalize. Good.
Number seeds: lengths maybe 5, each 4*5=20+5+3+1=29 ->145. ok.

But power with r=-0.5: right=n..1; right**(-0.5) at first=1/√n, last=1, shape decreasing? left x^-l. ok.
Ensure no zeros? x>=1, right>=1, positive. Good.

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=1800.0
    time_budget = min(budget_s - 60.0, 1740.0)
    # parent
    parent=None
    try:
       p=normalize_sequence(height_sequence_1)
       if p is not None and len(p) > 20:
           # If parent too long, downsample to 9000 to keep manageable
           if len(p) > 9000:
               p=interpolate_length(p, 9000)
           parent=p
           print('Parent length', len(parent))
    except Exception as e:
       print('No parent')
    lengths=[]
    default_lengths=[1280, 2560, 4096, 6200]

    if parent is not None:

       n_left = len(selected) - (len([i for ???])) # can't nested. Use index.

n_sel=len(selected)
for ii, sel in enumerate(selected):
   elapsed = time.monotonic()-t0
   if time.monotonic() >= start + time_budget - 200: break
   remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-start) - 200

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficients minimizing evaluate_sequence.

The score is scale-invariant and is internally optimized with FFT-based
linear self-convolutions (a*a).  The program builds many analytic seeds
(power-law endpoint shapes, ramps, random smooth shapes), then performs:
  - capping/spectral self-convolution flattening,
  - multiplicative subgradient (mirror) descent,
  - limited direct coordinate moves.
Finally, strong candidates are re-checked with the real evaluate_sequence
and the best valid sequence is returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a, cap=None):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values, optionally cap, normalize sum to 1."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if cap is not None:
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, float(cap))
    else:
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, np.inf)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def interpolate_length(a, new_n):
    """Interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    if n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    a = a / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s2 = float(out.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return out / s2


def fast_score(a, N=None):
    """
    Fast score for normalized non-negative sequence:
        2 * n * max_k conv(a,a)_k
    using FFT.  Returns (score, coefficients).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = A * A
    b = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if b.size == 0:
        M = 0.0
    else:
        M = float(np.max(b))
    return float(2.0 * n * M), b


def active_lags_weights(b, n_top, rel_tol):
    """Choose important convolution lags and soft weights."""
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=int), np.array([1.0], dtype=float), 0.0
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if n_top > b.size:
        n_top = b.size
    if n_top <= 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
        return idx, np.array([1.0], dtype=float), M

    top = np.argpartition(b, -n_top)[-n_top:]
    thresh = M * (1.0 - rel_tol)
    keep = top[b[top] >= thresh]
    if keep.size == 0:
        keep = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
    elif keep.size > n_top:
        keep = keep[:n_top]

    vals = b[keep]
    scale = max(1e-300, M * 0.05)
    w = np.exp((vals - M) / scale)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(keep.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(keep.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws
    return keep.astype(int), w.astype(float), M


def compute_gradient(a, ks, ws):
    """
    Subgradient of max selected coefficients b_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}.
    For one k, derivative w.r.t. a_j is 2 a_{k-j} on valid j.
    """
    n = len(a)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, w in zip(ks, ws):
        wi = float(w)
        if wi <= 0.0:
            continue
        ki = int(k)
        lo = ki - (n - 1)
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = ki
        if hi >= n:
            hi = n - 1
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        start = ki - lo
        stop = ki - hi - 1
        if stop < 0:
            stop = None
        grad[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * wi * a[start:stop:-1]
    return grad


def mirror_update(a, grad, q):
    """Multiplicative / mirror update: a_i <- exp(log a_i - q grad_i/max(grad))."""
    gmax = float(np.max(grad)) if grad.size else 0.0
    if gmax <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - q * (grad / gmax)
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
    a_new = np.exp(loga)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return a_new / s


def add_noise(a, rng, sigma):
    """Relative random perturbation preserving non-negativity and sum 1."""
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    p = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    a_new = a * (1.0 + p)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a
    return a_new / s


def spectral_from_b(b_cap, N, n):
    """
    Spectral flattening correction:
      target self-convolution coefficients b_cap -> spectrum B -> sqrt(B) -> time.
    Then project to support [0,n-1], non-negative, sum 1.
    """
    size = 2 * n - 1
    b_cap = np.asarray(b_cap, dtype=np.float64)
    if b_cap.size < size:
        tmp = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:b_cap.size] = b_cap
        b_cap = tmp
    else:
        b_cap = b_cap[:size]

    s = float(b_cap.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    b_cap = b_cap / s
    b_cap = np.maximum(b_cap, 0.0)

    buf = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    buf[:size] = b_cap

    Cpos = np.fft.rfft(buf, N)
    Cpos[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
    if N % 2 == 0:
        Cpos[-1] = abs(Cpos[-1]) + 0.0j

    mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(Cpos))
    ph = np.angle(Cpos)
    Apos = mag * (np.cos(0.5 * ph) + 1j * np.sin(0.5 * ph))
    Apos[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j

    a = np.fft.irfft(Apos, N)[:n]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s2


def spectral_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N):
    """Cap high convolution coefficients, spectrally square-root, project back."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = len(a)
    J, b = fast_score(a, N)
    best = a.copy()
    bestJ = J

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cap_frac = 0.95
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        avg = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
        t_target = max(avg, cap_frac * M)
        if t_target >= M:
            t_target = max(avg, 0.99 * M) if M > avg else M

        b_cap = np.minimum(b, t_target)
        b_cap = np.maximum(b_cap, 0.0)
        a_new = spectral_from_b(b_cap, N, n)

        if a_new is not None:
            J_new, b_new = fast_score(a_new, N)
            if np.isfinite(J_new) and J_new < J - 1e-12:
                a = a_new
                b = b_new
                J = J_new
                if J < bestJ - 1e-12:
                    best = a.copy()
                    bestJ = J
                    stall = 0
                cap_frac = max(0.82, cap_frac - 0.01)
            else:
                blend = 0.25
                a_blend = normalize_sequence((1.0 - blend) * a + blend * a_new)
                if a_blend is not None:
                    J_blend, b_blend = fast_score(a_blend, N)
                    if np.isfinite(J_blend) and J_blend < J - 1e-12:
                        a = a_blend
                        b = b_blend
                        J = J_blend
                        if J < bestJ - 1e-12:
                            best = a.copy()
                            bestJ = J
                            stall = 0
                    else:
                        stall += 1
                        cap_frac = min(0.99, cap_frac + 0.015)
                        if stall > 80:
                            stall = 0
                            cap_frac = 0.95
                            a = add_noise(a, rng, 0.03)
                            J, b = fast_score(a, N)
                            if J < bestJ:
                                bestJ = J
                                best = a.copy()
                else:
                    stall += 1
        else:
            stall += 1

        it += 1

    return best, bestJ


def mirror_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N, n_peaks=35):
    """Multiplicative subgradient descent on top convolution peaks."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    J, b = fast_score(a, N)
    best = a.copy()
    bestJ = J

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q = 0.6
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        top, w, M = active_lags_weights(b, int(n_peaks), 0.20)
        if top.size == 0:
            break
        grad = compute_gradient(a, top, w)
        gmax = float(np.max(grad)) if grad.size else 0.0
        if gmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        a_new = mirror_update(a, grad, q)
        J_new, b_new = fast_score(a_new, N)

        if np.isfinite(J_new) and J_new < J - 1e-12:
            a = a_new
            b = b_new
            J = J_new
            if J < bestJ - 1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                bestJ = J
                stall = 0
            q = min(4.0, q * 1.04)
        else:
            q = max(0.05, q * 0.85)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 400:
                a = add_noise(a, rng, 0.04)
                J, b = fast_score(a, N)
                stall = 0
                q = 0.6
                if J < bestJ:
                    bestJ = J
                    best = a.copy()

        it += 1

    return best, bestJ


def coordinate_phase(a0, deadline, seed, N):
    """Small number of direct coordinate moves attacking the current maximum."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = len(a)
    J, b = fast_score(a, N)
    best = a.copy()
    bestJ = J
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if b.size == 0:
            break
        k0 = int(np.argmax(b))
        lo = max(0, k0 - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k0)
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
        sz = idx.size
        if sz < 2:
            break

        prod = a[idx] * a[k0 - idx]
        src_pos = int(np.argmax(prod))
        src = int(idx[src_pos])

        sample = min(200, sz - 1)
        moved = False
        if sample > 0:
            low = np.argpartition(prod, sample)[:sample]
            candidates = []
            for pos in low:
                d = int(idx[pos])
                if d != src and a[d] < a[src]:
                    candidates.append(d)
            if candidates:
                dst = candidates[int(rng.integers(0, len(candidates)))]
                for frac in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
                    delta = a[src] * float(frac)
                    if delta <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    a_t = a.copy()
                    a_t[src] -= delta
                    a_t[dst] += delta
                    a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
                    s = float(a_t.sum())
                    if s <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    a_t = a_t / s
                    J_t, _ = fast_score(a_t, N)
                    if np.isfinite(J_t) and J_t < bestJ - 1e-12:
                        a = a_t
                        J = J_t
                        bestJ = J_t
                        best = a.copy()
                        b = fast_score(a, N)[1]
                        moved = True
                        stall = 0
                        break

        if not moved:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i != j and a[i] > 1e-20:
                delta = a[i] * float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08))
                a_t = a.copy()
                a_t[i] -= delta
                a_t[j] += delta
                a_t = np.maximum(a_t, 0.0)
                s = float(a_t.sum())
                if s > 1e-300:
                    a_t = a_t / s
                    J_t, _ = fast_score(a_t, N)
                    if np.isfinite(J_t) and J_t < J - 1e-12:
                        a = a_t
                        J = J_t
                        b = fast_score(a, N)[1]
                        if J < bestJ - 1e-12:
                            bestJ = J
                            best = a.copy()
                            stall = 0
                        else:
                            stall += 1
                    else:
                        stall += 1
                else:
                    stall += 1
            else:
                stall += 1

            if stall > 200:
                a = add_noise(a, rng, 0.05)
                J, b = fast_score(a, N)
                stall = 0

    return best, bestJ


def optimize_candidate(a0, t_limit, seed):
    """Sequential spectral, mirror, and coordinate polish for one candidate."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    t_limit = max(0.0, float(t_limit))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline_end = t0 + t_limit
    n = len(a)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best, bestJ = a.copy(), fast_score(a, N)[0]

    # Spectral phase
    spec_alloc = min(0.45 * t_limit, 180.0)
    spec_deadline = min(t0 + spec_alloc, deadline_end)
    if spec_alloc > 1.0 and time.monotonic() < spec_deadline:
        a_s, J_s = spectral_phase(a, spec_deadline, seed, N)
        if a_s is not None and np.isfinite(J_s) and J_s < bestJ:
            best = a_s.copy()
            bestJ = J_s

    # Mirror phase
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = t_limit - elapsed
    if rem > 20.0:
        mir_alloc = min(0.5 * rem, 240.0)
        mir_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + mir_alloc, deadline_end)
        a_m, J_m = mirror_phase(best, mir_deadline, seed + 101, N, n_peaks=35)
        if a_m is not None and np.isfinite(J_m) and J_m < bestJ:
            best = a_m.copy()
            bestJ = J_m

    # Coordinate phase
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = t_limit - elapsed
    if rem > 15.0:
        coord_alloc = min(0.30 * rem, 80.0)
        coord_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + coord_alloc, deadline_end)
        a_c, J_c = coordinate_phase(best, coord_deadline, seed + 202, N)
        if a_c is not None and np.isfinite(J_c) and J_c < bestJ:
            best = a_c.copy()
            bestJ = J_c

    return best, bestJ


def make_candidate_pool(parent, lengths):
    """Generate analytic seed sequences."""
    pool = []

    if parent is not None:
        pool.append(parent.copy())
        pn = len(parent)
        for mult in (0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5):
            nn = int(round(pn * mult))
            if 512 <= nn <= 9000:
                q = interpolate_length(parent, nn)
                if q is not None:
                    pool.append(q)

    for n in lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if n < 64:
            continue

        pool.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

        x = np.arange(1.0, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        right = np.arange(n, 0.0, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)

        # Endpoint power-law family: i^(-l) * (n-i+1)^r
        for l in (0.0, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
            left = x ** (-l)
            for r in (-0.50, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.50):
                a = left * (right ** r)
                q = normalize_sequence(a)
                if q is not None:
                    pool.append(q)

        # Simple ramps and smooth positive shapes
        for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
            for direction in (1, -1):
                xt = x if direction == 1 else right
                a = xt ** p
                q = normalize_sequence(a)
                if q is not None:
                    pool.append(q)

        # A few deterministic random smooth seeds
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1000 + n)
        for k in range(3):
            u = rng.random(n)
            u = np.clip(u, 1e-12, 1.0 - 1e-7)
            base = -np.log(1.0 - u)
            if k == 0:
                a = base * x
            elif k == 1:
                a = base * right
            else:
                a = base * x * right
            q = normalize_sequence(a)
            if q is not None:
                pool.append(q)

    cleaned = []
    for a in pool:
        q = normalize_sequence(a)
        if q is not None and 32 <= q.size <= 9000:
            cleaned.append(q)
    return cleaned


def safe_eval(a):
    """Call the external evaluator safely with a normalized sequence."""
    try:
        a = normalize_sequence(a, 1000.0)
        if a is None or len(a) == 0:
            return float("inf")
        # Sum is 1, well above evaluator threshold.  If it is somehow tiny, scale up.
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            a = a / s if s > 0 else a
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                a = np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)
        lst = [float(v) for v in a]
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)  # assumed already imported by the harness
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """Main search driver."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1800.0
    time_budget = min(max(budget_s - 90.0, 90.0), 1710.0)

    # Load previous construction, if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = normalize_sequence(height_sequence_1, 1000.0)
        if p is not None and p.size > 16:
            if p.size > 9000:
                p = interpolate_length(p, 9000)
            parent = p
            print("Loaded parent: len =", len(parent))
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        print("No parent sequence available.")

    # Candidate lengths.  Keep under 9000 for final np.convolve safety.
    base_lengths = [1024, 2048, 3072, 4096, 5120, 6144]
    if parent is not None:
        pn = len(parent)
        base_lengths.extend([pn, max(768, pn // 2), min(8192, pn + 1024)])
    lengths = []
    for n in base_lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if 512 <= n <= 9000 and n not in lengths:
            lengths.append(n)
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [4096]
    print("Using lengths:", lengths)

    # Build seeds and score them with fast FFT objective.
    pool = make_candidate_pool(parent, lengths)
    scored = []
    for a in pool:
        q = normalize_sequence(a)
        if q is None:
            continue
        nn = len(q)
        N = next_pow2(2 * nn - 1)
        J, _ = fast_score(q, N)
        if np.isfinite(J):
            scored.append((float(J), len(scored), q, N))

    if not scored:
        fallback = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        return [float(v) for v in fallback]

    # Scored tuples: (J, unique_id, array, N).  Tuple sort is safe because ids are unique.
    scored.sort()
    print("Best initial fast scores:", ["%.6f" % item[0] for item in scored[:10]])

    # Select a manageable list; always try parent as well.
    selected = []
    seen = set()
    if parent is not None:
        pa = normalize_sequence(parent)
        if pa is not None:
            pn = len(pa)
            Np = next_pow2(2 * pn - 1)
            Jp = fast_score(pa, Np)[0]
            selected.append((float(Jp), -1, pa, Np))
            seen.add(-1)

    max_selected = 8
    for item in scored:
        if len(selected) >= max_selected:
            break
        idx = item[1]
        if idx not in seen:
            selected.append(item)
            seen.add(idx)

    if not selected:
        selected = [scored[0]]

    best_fast = float(selected[0][0])
    best_seq = selected[0][2].copy()
    history = []
    hist_id = 0

    def remember(J, a):
        nonlocal hist_id
        if a is None or not np.isfinite(float(J)):
            return
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if a is None:
            return
        history.append((float(J), hist_id, a.copy()))
        hist_id += 1

    remember(best_fast, best_seq)

    # Allocation schedule (seconds) for the selected diverse candidates.
    alloc_schedule = [230, 210, 190, 170, 150, 130, 90, 90, 60, 60]
    reserve = min(210.0, 0.15 * time_budget)

    for ii, item in enumerate(selected):
        J0, idx, a0, N = item
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        remaining_all = time_budget - elapsed - reserve
        if remaining_all < 30.0:
            break
        base_alloc = alloc_schedule[ii] if ii < len(alloc_schedule) else 50.0
        alloc = min(float(base_alloc), max(30.0, remaining_all / 2.0))
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break

        print("Optimizing candidate %d, n=%d, initJ=%.6f, alloc=%.0fs" %
              (ii, len(a0), J0, alloc))
        a_opt, J_opt = optimize_candidate(a0, alloc, 555 + 17 * ii)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if a_opt is not None and np.isfinite(J_opt):
            remember(J_opt, a_opt)
            if J_opt < best_fast - 1e-12:
                best_fast = float(J_opt)
                best_seq = a_opt.copy()
                print("  New fast best: %.6f (n=%d, t=%.0fs)" %
                      (best_fast, len(best_seq), time.monotonic() - start))

    # Multi-length continuation from the current best.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    remaining_all = time_budget - elapsed - 60.0
    if best_seq is not None and remaining_all > 120.0:
        bn = len(best_seq)
        trial_ns = []
        for nn in (max(768, bn // 2), bn + 1024, min(8192, bn + 2048), 8192):
            nn = int(nn)
            if 512 <= nn <= 9000 and nn not in trial_ns:
                trial_ns.append(nn)

        for nn in trial_ns:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
            remaining_all = time_budget - elapsed - 45.0
            if remaining_all < 80.0:
                break
            if nn == bn:
                continue
            a_int = interpolate_length(best_seq, nn)
            if a_int is None:
                continue
            Nnn = next_pow2(2 * nn - 1)
            Ji = fast_score(a_int, Nnn)[0]
            remember(Ji, a_int)
            if Ji < best_fast - 1e-12:
                best_fast = Ji
                best_seq = a_int.copy()
                print("Interpolated length %d improves fast score to %.6f" % (nn, best_fast))

            alloc = min(150.0, max(45.0, remaining_all - 45.0))
            a_opt, J_opt = optimize_candidate(a_int, alloc, 9000 + nn)
            if a_opt is not None and np.isfinite(J_opt):
                remember(J_opt, a_opt)
                if J_opt < best_fast - 1e-12:
                    best_fast = float(J_opt)
                    best_seq = a_opt.copy()
                    print("  Multi-length new fast best: %.6f (n=%d, t=%.0fs)" %
                          (best_fast, nn, time.monotonic() - start))

    # Final validation with the real evaluator.  Limit count/length to stay within time.
    best_safe_seq = None
    best_safe_score = float("inf")
    use_real_eval = False

    # Always remember the global fast best one more time.
    remember(best_fast, best_seq)

    hist_scored = []
    for J, hid, a in history:
        if np.isfinite(J):
            hist_scored.append((float(J), hid, a))
    hist_scored.sort()

    checked = 0
    for J, hid, a in hist_scored:
        if checked >= 7:
            break
        nn = len(a)
        if nn > 9500:
            continue
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - start) - 8.0
        if rem < 6.0:
            break
        sc = safe_eval(a)
        checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            use_real_eval = True
            if sc < best_safe_score - 1e-12:
                best_safe_score = float(sc)
                best_safe_seq = a.copy()
                print("Real evaluator checked n=%d score=%.6f" % (nn, sc))

    if use_real_eval and best_safe_seq is not None:
        final_seq = best_safe_seq
        print("FINAL using real evaluator: score=%.6f, n=%d" %
              (best_safe_score, len(final_seq)))
    else:
        final_seq = best_seq
        print("Finaler using fast fallback: score=%.6f, n=%d" %
              (best_fast, len(final_seq)))

    final_seq = normalize_sequence(final_seq)
    if final_seq is None:
        final_seq = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0

    return [float(v) for v in final_seq]
```
